                                              Contract No.: POGC 717-90-35

                    21.3. DRILLING HAZARDS

               •  Stuck Pipe (tight hole)
                         -  Geopressured or unstable fractured shale found in both the Sudair and Aghar
                      Shale sections.
                         -  Over gauge hole in offset wells indicated 12”–15” in the Sudair and 15”–20”
                          in the Aghar shale formations.
                         -  Stuck pipe on connections have occurred as a result of poor hole cleaning and
                     sudden pack off due to hole collapse.
                         -  Stuck casing running due to collapse of formation.

               •  Differential Pressure (Stuck Pipe)
               As a result of different formation pressure top to bottom 1.15 SG to 1.4 SG
                       this can instigate loss circulation as well as differential sticking of the drill string.

               •   Drill string twist off and or Wash Outs

                 High potential of twist off due to the excessive vibration and presence of H2S.
                   Maintain equipment inspection at all times.

               •  Salt Water Flow (Over pressured Sudair-Dashtak Formation)
                      Salt water flow has been experienced SP-7 calculated at 1.85 SG. High chance it is
                     localized high pressure low volume.

               •  Loss Circulation
                Surmeh and Neyriz formations have shown areas of potential losses as a result of
                       drilling fluid being higher than 1.15 SG. Possibly caused by faults, fractures and or
                   porous formation.

               •  Low ROP
                   Hith Formation below 13-3/8” casing shoe.

                 21.8.    DIRECTIONAL DRILLING

                  •   The plan for the section is to drill with 2 bit runs.
                  •   Section TD will be +/‐ 10 m TVD into the Upper Kangan Formation.

                  •   This section will be vertical and the plan is to hold inclination at
                   approximately less than 2 degree to Section TD.

                  •   The BHA will include 8-1/2 or 8” motor, PBL sub(optional) and jar/accelerator.

                  •   Other down hole tools such as  drill pipe torque reducer (DPTR) and or
                  harmonic tool both maybe incorporated in to the BHA assemblies all in efforts to
                   reduce the excessive torque and vibration on the  drill  string. Using these tools
              may  also enhance performance and thus increased ROP and directional drilling
                     control.


                 21.9.    SURVEYING

                 No TOTCO surveys planned for this hole section
                 Gyro survey could be run after running & cementing

                    9-5/8” X 10-3/4” CSG.

                           INTERVAL DISCUSION

                   This interval consists of limestone, clay, marl, shale, sand stone, siltstone, dolomite

                 and anhydrite.

                The 12 ¼ -inch interval will be drilled with a KCl /Polymer mud system. The fluid

                     will be carried over from the previous section at zero cost. The utilization of a KCl

                  /Polymer mud system should firmly inhibit shale(s) and provides excellent properties

                      drilling and inhibition properties.

                   This section will cross the HITH, SURMEH, DASHTAK, SUDAIR and AGHAR

                   formations and casing will be set into KANGAN. These formations consist of

                  Dolomite, Limestone, Chert, Anhydrites and shale(s). Before drilling the 13 3/8”

                  Casing shoe, the mud should be treated with sodium bicarbonate and citric acid to

                   avoid cement contamination. During  drilling  this  interval KCl and Sulfonated

                   Asphalt are the primary components providing inhibition to the shale sections.

                  Ensure that concentrations are as per program and that these are determined and

                    reported every tour.

                     Sufficient and adequate stocks of LCM are to be kept on board of the rig as partial to

                   severe losses are expected in SURMEH formations. In case of seepage losses, the

                  system should be treated with finer grade of LCM, for Moderate to large Losses,

                   prepare pills at different F, M, C concentrations and spot across the thief zone.

                    In the event of severe losses, cross linking pills should be pumped to minimize

                      losses.

                  There is the possibility of Salt Water flow in DASHTAK formation .Regarding to

                  Experience in the South Pars Oil Field, the water analysis of the influx in offset wells

                  gave a brine weight of 1.165 S.G. containing high concentrations of Calcium 12800

                    mg/l, Magnesium 10800 mg/l, Sodium 104000 mg/l and Chloride 163000 mg/l. This

                 had caused severe contamination to the mud, which necessitated a lot of dump and

                     dilution and high products consumptions. Therefore, sufficient stocks of products are

                     to be on Board to control flow. Mud weight was increased to 2.0 S.G. to control

                    flow, but well remained flowing between 4 - 7 m³/hr till section TD in one of the

                      offset wells. The possibility of  differential sticking  is SURMEH formation, to

                  minimize it, tighten fluid loss and introduce Seepage loss control material into the

                   system. Seepage loss control material will bridge and seal formation hence reduces

                      differential sticking. It is important in AGHAR shale to maintain the mud weigh at

                   1.41 S.G. and reduce the API fluid loss to less than 2 ml and provide sufficient

                     inhibition of KCl and Sulfonated Asphalt. This should provide optimum hole

                       stability and less hole problems. However, as a contingency, if extra inhibition is

                    required in AGHAR consideration should be given to Glycol and PHPA.

                 12 1/4” Section Run finest possible shaker screens and optimize solids removal

                  equipment at all times in order to reduce dilution volume, thus reducing mud cost.

                            12-1/4” Hole Section Drilling Fluid Out Line

                  Keep Mud Weight as low as possible to 1.12 S.G until passing Surmeh,(refer to

                     engineering comments on drilling Surmeh-Lithiotis mbr).

                   Increase MW gradually from 1.12-1.18 S.G prior to entering neyriz formation.

              POTENTIAL MUD RELATED PROBLEMS AND HAZARDS.

                Well bore and formation related problems are identified as follows:

                      Hole instability in the Upper SUDAIR Shale section.

                       Partial to severe loss of circulation in SURMEH formation.

                       Sensitive and reactive shale(s) in the AGHAR formation.

                       Salt Water influx in the DASHTAK formation.

               Hole instability: Maintain MW as per pore pressure curve dictates in the drilling program.

               The mud weight might be raised up to 1.36 S.G. prior to entering the Upper SUDAIR Shale.

                 Maintain KCl and Sulfonated Asphalt as per program to inhibit the shale.

                          It is important in AGHAR shale to keep MW at >1.41 S.G., fluid loss < 2 cc's and provide

                     sufficient inhibition of KCl and Sulfonated Asphalt. This should provide optimum hole stability

                and less hole problems.

                   In the event extra inhibition is required for stabilizing the AGHAR shale(s), additions of PHPA

                   are to be considered. Glycol  is a poly-alkaline Glycol, which will chemically inhibit the

                  swelling of shale(s) by interfering with the buildup of water hydra ionic layers. PHPA is a shale

                    inhibitor and acts as encapsulating polymer, minimizing the hydration and dispersion of

                    cuttings.

               Lost circulation: The concentration of LCM will vary according to losses. Partial to severe

                   losses are expected in SURMEH formations and sufficient stocks of graded LCM should be

                  kept on board. In case of seepage losses, the system should be treated with fine grade of LCM,

                    for moderate to severe losses, prepare pills at different graded concentrations and spot across

                   the thief zone.

                   Inhibition and hole stability:

                 Shale Inhibition: Potassium chloride and Sulfonated Asphalt will be the primary and secondary

                    inhibitors for this fluid. It would be an option to start off with a slightly higher Concentration of

              KCl to avoid catching up when the dilution volume requirements are high.

               The cuttings need to be monitored for firmness and integrity at the shakers. If these dictate, the

                  Concentrations of KCl and/or Sulfonated Asphalt may need to be increased.

          Extra inhibition: is required in AGHAR consideration should be given to Glycol and PHPA.

             KCl Content: If cuttings dictate, increase KCl content.

                 Sulfonated Asphalt content: If hole conditions dictate, increase Sulfonated Asphalt content.

               Use Sulfonated Asphalt from the beginning of the section, it will lubricate the cake thus reduce

                   torque, and decrease fluid loss. In the event of drag and torque, add a lubricant.

                Both surface and sub surface to be documented on a daily basis.

                   across thief zone. Circulation sub could be installed to avoid LCM plugging MWD tools (MWD

                  engineer is to advise on the size and concentration of LCM that can be pumped through MWD

                   Tools). In the event of severe losses, cross-linking pills should be pumped to minimize losses.

                   Differential sticking: The potential of differential is present after SURMEH formation, to

                 minimize  it, tighten fluid loss and introduce 14 kg/m³ seepage loss control into the system.

                Seepage loss control will bridge and seal formation hence reduces differential sticking.

                   Salt Water Influx: Possibility of Salt Water Influx may occur in DASHTAK formation. This

                 caused severe contamination due to high percentage of Calcium, Magnesium and Chloride.

                    Sufficient stock must be at rig site to control flow and to treat mud from mixed salt flow

                  contamination.

                Hydraulics: During the drilling of this interval detailed Hydraulics modeling will take place

                on a daily basis using the Virtual Hydraulics software programmers. This programmer will

                model the fluid behavior under down hole conditions and give predictions of hole cleaning

                   efficiency and ECD values. Rheology Virtual Hydraulics has a module specifically designed to

                model the performance of the system.

                Hole cleaning: Prior to tripping pipe, the hole should be circulated clean and, if possible, the

                       drill pipe rotated.

                  Solids control: to be optimized with on board shale shakers, desilter and centrifuge.

                   Solids control equipment: Use all available solids control equipment efficiently to maintain mud

                  weight. Adapt a dump and dilute strategy, using whole pre-mix mud, to control the weight.

                 Shale shakers: Ensure shakers are running immediately prior to and during all periods of

                   Circulation .A shaker to be dressed initially with 80/100 meshes screens and after the mud is

                   sheared, finer screens of 150/180 mesh should be dressed to avoid making gumbos while

                     drilling through the shale sections.

     REV.: 01                          Doc. No.: DDC-12-14-RE-005-01
DATE: 26 FEB. 2013                      Contract No.: POGC 717-90-35                 Page 113 of 169

           LOST CIRCULATION STRATEGY:

                      •   The Surmeh/Arab Formation major area for loss circulation to occur.
                               Difficult to control losses in this formation.

                      •   Whenever breaking circulation increase pump stroke rate in steps and
                            move/rotate drill string slowly not aggressively.

                      •    If any over pull/pack-off occurs whenever reaming upwards move
                                    drill string down and reduce pump rate to avoid breaking down additional
                             formation.

                      •   Have LCM material on board in case required and make up small pill in
                             readiness to be used. Emphasis is to allow the formation to heal itself
                            before any other option will be considered i.e. Cement.

                      •  A multiple circulating sub(PBL sub - Optional) is planned so  that if
                 LCM is required to be used it will not plug up the MWD tool.

                      •   Attempt to spot fine LCM pill through MWD tool and if losses still exist
                          drop the ball so that a coarser LCM pill can be spotted. Evaluate to continue
                               to drill ahead before dropping the ball.

                      •   Try to cure the losses do not pump more than two LCM  pills  if
                            unsuccessful as the strategy is the formation will cure itself.

                      •  No cement is planned at any time to cure the losses.

               Engineering Comments to minimize Risk of Heavy loss in Lithiotis member(base
                          of surmeh)


             Having Enough Mud Volume in Mud Pits prior to drill

                          into Lithiotis mbr.

             Keep MW as low as possible until passing Lithiotis

                      mbr(1.12 S.G), by Dump & Dilute.

            Add LCM CACO3 (F+M), 1+1 SX per Hour and Mica

                  F ,1 SX per Hour, 10 hours prior to drill into Lithiotis

                     mbr.

             At Top of Lithiotis mbr, circulate BTM’s UP to reduce

           MW in annulus.

             Reduce GPM to minimum +/- 600-650 GPM until bit

                      passed through lithiotis mbr.

               Engineering Comments to minimize risk of Bit balling from S8 to Aghar Shale

              After drilling every 5 m from S8 to base of Aghar

                      shale,Backream and ream the drilled interval, Circulate

                          off bottom for 15 Minutes then resume drilling.

                  Drilling Program of Vertical Well(Learning Well)
                             SPD18B-08




     REV.: 01                          Doc. No.: DDC-12-14-RE-005-01
DATE: 26 FEB. 2013                      Contract No.: POGC 717-90-35                 Page 115 of 169


                      STUCK PIPE STRATEGY:

                           •    Neyriz, Sudair (S8) and Aghar Shale: Stuck pipe has occurred
                                   majority of time when making a connection which is due to poor
                                   cleaning and sudden pack off/hole collapse.

                           •    Heavy back reaming could be required.

                           •    Always monitor speed and momentum of back reaming aggressive
                            may have to be toned down to lessen the collapse effect.

                           •   A smooth profile is required a spiral tight effect limits FM
                                       degradation.

                           •     Differential sticking has occurred Arab and Surmeh zones. Keep
                                low fluid loss to limit the sticking. Minimize drill string
                                     connection time.

                           •    Always rotate, reciprocate/pump on whenever operations are
                                      stopped.

                           •     Utilize  a  Turbo-Back  stabilizer b e t w e e n m o t o r  and
                             P D C   bit f o r smoothing the profile.

                           •      If OP/pack off occurs when reaming up move pipe down and
                            reduce pump rate immediately to avoid fracturing the formation.

                           •      If mechanically stuck start jarring at a low force and gradually
                                        increase to limit pack off.

                           •    Never jar up with any torque in the drill string. Immediately hit
                                              jar upwards with maximum allowable over pull.

                           •    Always hit jar in opposite direction to getting stuck.

                           •     Drillers should all be aware of the maximum pull on jar and drill
                                         string.

                           •    Use jar in combination with accelerator in all BHAs.

                            •    Geologist should evaluate maximum pore pressure and potential
                                   permeable zones to verify weight reduction.

                           •    Minimize the casing running time when crossing over from 9-5/8”
                                      to 10-3/4”.

                           •    Have HCL acid on the rig in case of differential sticking through
                                          section.

                  Drilling Program of Vertical Well(Learning Well)

     REV.: 01                          Doc. No.: DDC-12-14-RE-005-01

                      DRILLING PITFALLS

                       •  Losses in the Surmeh Formation
                                    -  Mud weight 1.12 SG
                                    -  Follow connection procedures
                                    -  Always ream down with reduced pump rate 2.5 m3/min

                       •  Differential Sticking in the Surmeh Formation
                                    -  Focus on connection time ensure crew prepared for quick connection
                                    -  Add up to 2 -3 % KCL to as shale inhibitor to the mud
                                    -  Immediately hit jar upwards with maximum allowable over pull.
                                    -  Take surveys before any connection 1 single off bottom to be able
                                  to work pipe down if stuck.
                                    -  At all times never leave the drill string motionless always rotate pipe.

                       •  Damage of bit in Dashtak Upper Dolomite
                        Reduce drilling parameters when drilling in Upper Dolomite

                       •  Water kick in the Sudair Formation
                          Ensure good Volume control and Kick detection

                       •  Gas in Dashtak
                          Connection and trip gas experienced in SP-9

                       •  Mechanical Sticking in Sudeir S8 & Aghar shales

                         Follow separate procedures

                       •  Differential Sticking in Surmeh


                 21.11.      9 5/8" X 10 3/4" CASING SET UP

                      The recommended 9⅝" casing is 47LBS/FT and L80 grade material with Vam Top or

                            equivalent connections. The recommended 10¾" casing is 55.5 LBS/FT and L-80

                            material with Vam Top or equivalent connections. will be set at +/- 180mRTE. One

                       X.O.S 9-5/8”,47#,Vam TOP or Equiv. X 10-3/4”, 55.5 #, Vam Top or Equiv. is

                             required.

                     Shoe track

              9 5/8” float shoe (47#, L-80); 2 joints of 9 5/8” casing; 9 5/8” float collar (L-80).

              21.12.        CASING ACCESSORIES

                         All Centralizers to be installed on the body of CSG with Stop collar inside

                       the Centralizers.

                            Thread Lock Float shoe & float collar

                              Install one bow Centralizer on the first 2 joints of CSG.

                           One bow centralizer every two joints on the first 10 joints above the shoe.

                              Install 1 centralizer every 5 joints in open hole & Cased Hole.

                           Two rigid centralizers (9-5/8” to fit with the ID of 13-3/8” CSG) to be

                        installed on joint No. 3 & 4 below CSG HGR  in order to keep the 9-5/8” casing

             HGR centered inside the unihead.13-3/8” X 9-5/8” rigid centralizers on first three

                        joints of 9-5/8” inside the 13-3/8” casing.

                          1 Stop Collar should be installed inside each centralizers on the Body of

                     CSG(install stop collars on the casing’s prior to PU casing joints to the rig floor).

                 Premium Connection casing will be run and pressure tested to 4000 psi. After

         21.14.   PRODUCTION CSG 9-5/8” X 10-3/4” CEMENTING PROCEDURES


                       •  The objective of this cement job is ensure that there is a solid shoe anti gas
                           migration cement job and that there is isolation in the 13-3/8” x 9-5/8”
                           annulus to prevent hydrocarbons and any sulfurous water flowing
                              potential to surface.

                       •  The cement slurry composition will be a low weight lead slurry at 1.4-
                           1.46 SG and an anti gas migration tail slurry at 1.9 SG.

                    22. 8‐1/2” HOLE SECTION (7” PRODUCTION LINER)


                           22.1. DRILLING OBJECTIVES

                    The 8-1/2” hole will be drilled to 30 m MD after target point and target point
                               is the lower perforation point which is 30 m minimum above the estimated
                GWC. However, the GWC varies across the field, and will be given for each
                         individual well. The Section will be completed using cemented 7” liner.


                           22.2. DRILLING EVENT SUMMARY

                           1.  Prepare new CACO3-polymer mud(DO NOT USE BARITE in MUD
                 SYSTEM)
                           2.  Test BOP
                           3.  Install wear bushing
                           4. L/D bit and any excess of BHA components from the previous section. P/U
             & stand back in the derrick enough 5 1/2” or 5 7/8” drill pipes to reach the well
                   TD.
                           5.  Ensure that adequate enough amount of mud chemicals,Baryte(contingency),
                     cement and cement additives and also H2S scavenger are available on site.
                           6.  Ensure that all the personnel on site are aware of risks connected with the
                          potential presence of H2S and they are trained on using the BA system. Run
                 H2S alarm drills and check their preparedness.
                           7.  M/U 8½” bit and RIH BHA.
                           8.  Press. Test CSG if not already Done
                           9.  Perform Well Control, H2S and and burner boom drill prior to drill out shoe
                        10. Drill out shoe track and clean rat hole. Drill 3 m new formation.
                        11. Displace hole to new CACO3 mud,-DO NOT USE BARYTE in MUD
                 SYSTEM.
                        12. perform FIT to 1.6 S.G Mud.
                        13. POOH.
                        14. RUN CBL-VDL-CCL-GR from 9-5/8” CSG(to be confirmed), RIH BHA
                        15. Drill well to TD.
                       Note: Mud density must be kept constant throughout the phase. Be aware that
                        pressure gradient is higher at top reservoir and then decreases towards the
                     bottom with the presence of H2S and CO2 gases. Although circulation losses are




        This Document Is The Sole Property Of D.D.C. All Information Contained Herein May Neither Be Reproduced,Used,
         Disclosed, Transmitted Or In Any Way Made Public Without The Written Permission Of The D.D.C.

                  Drilling Program of Vertical Well(Learning Well)
                             SPD18B-08




     REV.: 01                          Doc. No.: DDC-12-14-RE-005-01
DATE: 26 FEB. 2013                      Contract No.: POGC 717-90-35                 Page 124 of 169


                         unlikely to happen, be ready to take all the necessary precautions to control such
                       event to avoid having gas up at surface
                        16. Mud pumps are to be brought up slowly to minimize hydraulic shock on the
                        formations; pipes must be rotated slowly in an effort to break the mud gels.
                        17. Perform short trips as dictated by hole conditions. All trips off bottom
                       should be approached with caution to avoid hole swabbing.(pump out of hole to
                           limit swab effect)
                        18. BOP tests will be performed according to BOP Testing Frequency &
                        Procedures.
                        19. At total depth, circulate clean hole. Wiper trip to 9 5/8” casing shoe. On
                     bottom check for tight spots and/or fill. If required the wiper trip will be
                        repeated to condition hole properly. If hole is OK: circulate & condition the
                    mud.
                        20. POOH, pump out
                        21. Run open hole logs from 8-1/2” hole
                        22. Wiper trip
                        23. Run 7” liner with liner hanger Assy. and set at 120 m above 9 5/8” CSG
                    Shoe & cement liner the 7" production liner will be cemented up to the liner
                      hanger using neat slurry
                        24. RIH with 8 1/2“ bit drill out cement to top of liner.
                        25. RIH with 5 7/8” bit drill out wiper plug to top of landing caller.
                        26. Run CBL-VBL-CCL-GR from 7” or 9-5/8” CSG
                        27. Run Completion(to be advised in due time)


                           22.3. DRILLING HAZARDS

                   H2S
                                  High values of H2S while drilling is not expected based on offset
                                         wells.
                                  During  testing and  production  the  values  of H2S  in  the
                                         reservoir can be up to 5000 ppm.
                         Well Control
                                  Any well control situation, Dana Kish Drilling Well Control
                                    Procedures  will follow  the API 54 and 63 Standard Well
                                     Control – ‘Drillers Method’.
                                  Operations will be stopped whenever severe losses occur.

                  Drilling Program of Vertical Well(Learning Well)
                             SPD18B-08




     REV.: 01                          Doc. No.: DDC-12-14-RE-005-01
DATE: 26 FEB. 2013                      Contract No.: POGC 717-90-35                 Page 125 of 169


                                   Drill string will be pumped out of hole for ANY  trip’s to
                                      prevent and eliminate swabbing effect.
                                  Monitor all gas levels; trip, connection and background.
                    •  Differential Sticking
                                  The K4 reservoir could be depleted up to 450 psi. Resulting in
                                         to approximately 1000‐1450 psi. Overbalance. Also K4 has the
                                       highest permeability.
                                   If differential sticking occurs: Evaluate to pull out with the
                       BHA  and  drill the remaining with a rotary BHA without
                         PDM/MWD/LWD.
                                  Surveys in K4: Quick Survey at all times (hole condition
                                           dictated).
                                  Keep acid (HCL) on one of the rigs.

                       •  Losses
                                  Losses generally do not occur and are indicated through seepage
                                     only one well has recorded  substantial  losses and  that  is
                                 SPD2‐05.

                       •  Packing off
                                  Consider to reduce flow rate to 5.0 m3/m when back ream
                                     through shale sections

                       •   Potential Hole Swabbing while Trip Out

                                  Consider Pump Out of Hole specilally in K4

                           22.8. DIRECTIONAL DRILLING


                   •   Primary objective is to drill this hole section using one BHA.

                   •   This section will be vertical

                   •   The BHA will be designed to minimize wall contact and diff. sticking (i.e.
                minimum DC’s)
                   •   The BHA will include 6‐3/4” motor, 8-1/4 Stab. and Jar/Accelerator

                   •   Total depth (TD) will be 30 m MD below target point.


                           22.9. SURVEYING

                   •  No TOTCO Survey planned for this hole section

                   •   Gyro Survey can be run after running and cementing 7” Liner

                     22.10.    DRILLING FLUID PROGRAM

                    OBJECTIVES
                                Use salt saturated polymer brine mud system using CaCO3 as both a
                                   bridging agent as well as a weighting material.

                               NO BARITE should be used for any reason.

                               Mud weight should begin at no lower than 1.41 SG.

                                Use KCL Polymer mud

                                CaCO3 will be used both as a bridging agent as well as fluid loss control
                                  agent.

                                Add corrosion inhibitor and oxygen scavenger.

                                Have pipe lax available

                                  All mixing done through the hopper.

                                Key Products: KCl, NaCl, PAC, Xanthan Gum, Caustic Soda, Soda Ash

              MUD COMPOSITION

                 Sea water …………………… 800 - 1000 mg/l Calcium.
               KCl …………………………..… Inhibition
                NaCl ………………………….. Inhibition
                   Caustic soda……………..... pH controls.
                 Soda Ash………………….… Hardness control.
                 Xanthan Gum…………….. Rheological control
                 High quality Starch……….Fluid loss control
             PAC L…………………………. Fluid loss control.
             PAC R …………………..…... Viscosifier / Fluid loss control.
           CaCO3…………………….. Weighting agent.
                   Biocide
                 Defoamer

           Solids control

                         Solids control: to be optimized with on board shale shaker and centrifuge.
                         Solids control equipment: Use all available solids control equipment
                               efficiently to maintain mud weight. Adapt a dump and dilute strategy, using
                       whole pre-mix mud, to control the weight
                        Shale shakers: Ensure shakers are running immediately prior to and during
                                    all periods of Circulation. Shakers to be dressed initially with 80/100 mesh
                          screens and after the mud is sheared, finer screens of 170/250 mesh should
                      be dressed to avoid making gumbos while drilling through the shale sections.


            Drilling / Fluids Out line

                               1. Displacement: Ensure mud has been pre-treated for cement
                             contamination with sodium bicarbonate
                               2. Controlled drilling: Strongly recommended for proper hole cleaning.
                                Cuttings should be circulated up above the BHA prior to connections. It
                             should also be considered to ream at each connection.
                               3. This will minimize the potential of cuttings settling during connections,
                               causing packing off and stuck pipe. Prior to trips the hole should be
                                 circulated clean and at least 1 ½ x bottoms-up. This will prevent tight

                              hole on the way out and bridges, fill etc. on the way back in.
                               4. Dump and dilute strategy: Dilute active system with premixed fluid to
                              maintain volume and mud properties. The concentration of this fluid
                             should be somewhat higher than in the initial mix to compensate for
                                depletion. Experience has also proved that addition of Defoamer to the
                            premix is more efficient than directly into the active system if foaming
                                           is a problem in the active system.


          8 ½" INTERVAL DISCUSSION

                   This section should be drilled with a close to salt saturated Drill-In mud system to

                  minimize solids invasion into the producing formation. His mud system will be built

                 from Scratch due to presence of Barite and Sulfonated Asphalt into the previous mud

                  system which will cause damages to the reservoir section.

                     Salt saturation will be provided by both Potassium and Sodium Chloride. The

                  chemical tests should be performed on the mud.

                   Sized Calcium carbonate should be encountered in the mud system to assist in

                    bridging the formation porosities to minimize the solids invasion into the producing

                    formation. The fluid loss should be controlled at <2 ml/30min.

             Mud weight shall be kept as low as possible to avoid any Barite addition. If the mud

                  system required more weight  it can be provided by addition of more calcium

                   carbonate to 1.41 S.G. For further density increase usage of Barite is unavoidable.

                The provision of keeping onboard enough and  sufficient quantity of  barite as

                   contingency should be considered. This will help in case of a need of increasing to

                    higher mud densities.

               As this section calls for long directional section there may be the requirement for

                     additional  lubricants while  drilling  the  interval.  Additional  lubricants  will be

                     available at the rig site during this interval.

                   This interval will be drilled in KANGAN and DALAN reservoir formation. The

                   formations are divided into four major sequences called K1, 2, 3 and 4. The reservoir

                     consists mainly of dolomites plus a lower content of limestone and minor anhydrite

                 and calystone.

                     Prior to drilling cement treat mud with sodium bicarbonate and citric acid to avoid

                 cement Contamination.

                  Maintain MW at 1.35-1.41 S.G. throughout this interval. KCl should provide the

                   necessary inhibition to the low clay content. Ensure KCl content is as per program

                 and is determined and reported every tour.

                     Sufficient and adequate stocks of acid soluble LCM are to be kept on board of the rig

                    as partial to severe losses are expected in K2 and K3 formations. In case of seepage

                      losses, the system should be treated with finer grade of LCM, for Moderate to large

                    Losses, prepare pills at different F, M, C concentrations and spot across the thief

                   zone.

                    In the event of severe losses, cross linking pills should be pumped to minimize

                      losses.

                The possibility of differential sticking is present in K4 formation, to minimize  it,

                   maintain fluid loss less than 2 ml and introduce seepage loss control material into the

                POTENTIAL MUD RELATED PROBLEMS AND HAZARDS
                 Well bore and formation related problems are identified as follows:

                        Differential sticking in KHUFF formation, especially K4.

                       Possible losses in K2 and K3.

                       Present of H2S, especially in K1

                          should be focused:

                      •  Experienced ECD: 1.57 – 1.65 SG

                      •      Hole cleaning / cutting trend plot

             LOST CIRCULATION STRATEGY

                       •   Only seepage losses have been observed in SPD1 and SPD2 wells, except
                                for SPD2-05 where significant losses had in the K1 formation.

                              •   Whenever breaking circulation increase pump stroke rate in steps and
                             move/rotate drill string slowly not aggressively.

                              •      If any over pull/pack-off occurs whenever reaming upwards move drill
                                  string down and reduce pump rate to avoid breaking down additional
                               formation.

                              •   Have LCM material on board in case required and make up small pills in
                              readiness to be used. Emphasis is to allow the formation to heal itself
                             before any other option will be considered i.e. Cement last resort. Refer
                                to the decision tree and LCM pill makeup and concentration for when and
                      how to go forward when losses are indicated.

                              •  PBL Sub is planned to use in the string in case of losses and the need of
                        pumping course LCM materials.

                              •  Try to cure  the  losses do  not pump more  than two LCM  pills  if
                            unsuccessful as the strategy is the formation will cure itself.

                         •  No cement is planned at any time to cure the losses.

                                   •    Possible losses in K2 and K3.


                     The concentration of LCM will vary according to losses. Partial to severe losses are

                         expected in K2 and K3 formations and sufficient stocks of acid soluble graded LCM,

                  Drilling Program of Vertical Well(Learning Well)
                             SPD18B-08




     REV.: 01                          Doc. No.: DDC-12-14-RE-005-01
DATE: 26 FEB. 2013                      Contract No.: POGC 717-90-35                 Page 137 of 169


                        Keep acid (HCl) on the rig in case a differential sticking situation occurs.
                         Utilize “Turbo Back” stabilizer between motor and the bit to prevent
                           hole spiraling, by limiting the side cutting action of the bit and smoothing
                             the profile of the wellbore.
                         If differential stuck, start jarring at max hit on the jar to prevent
                         worsening the situation.
                        Never apply full over pull before allowing the jar to hit.
                        After the jar has hit, max DP over pull to be applied.
                        Never jar up with torque in string.
                        Always hit jar in opposite direction getting stuck (most likely the jar
                          should be hit downwards).
                         Driller shall be aware of max pull on jar and drill string.
                        Use jar in combination with accelerator in all BHAs.

                          In order to reduce and minimize  this occurrence, maintain fluid loss and

                        introduce seepage loss control material into the system. Liquid Casing will

                        bridge and seal formation hence reduces differential sticking.


               DRILLING PITFALLS

                       •   Differential sticking
                                    -  Keep fluid loss below 2 ml.
                                    -  Minimize connection time
                                    -  When stuck jar immediately at maximum hit upwards.
                                    -  Apply maximum torque string and set down maximum weight
                                    -  Always rotate and pump if drill string stationary.

                       •  Swabbing
                                    -  Follow drill and ream parameters.
                                    -  Never pull upwards without pumping.
                                    -  Monitor trip tank volumes and active volumes.
                                    -  Pump into slips and take high connection drilling then POOH.

                   1.      Tally, number, clean, drift and prepare the 7” 29 and 26 ppf, liner as it is being
                         offloaded. Prepare casing Tally sheet prior to running Include detailed running
                     and plug head dimensions for accurate space out and displacement considerations.
                  Run x joints 29 ppf,L-80 Premium connection followed by one joint 26 ppf, 110
                      KSI, 28% CRA Premium Connection to 120 m inside the 9‐5/8” casing(120 m
                    O.Lap is considered).

                   2.    Measure and record the length and ID of the 7” liner running tool.
                   3.    Have the drill pipe crossover to cement head and liner HGR Running
                    Tool.
                   4.     Prior to start RIH make Up Liner HGR to XOS and Cement Manifold to pup
                           joints and XOS below and top  (if required) and L/D same.
            NOTE: Ensure the liner running tool is premade up in Kish prior to delivery to
                                       drilling unit.

                   5.     Proper sizes of XOS to be ordered

                   6.     Rig up to run the 7” liner.
                              -If liner running tool is not premade up in Kish make up liner hanger running
                           tool in liner hanger and L/D.
                       -Ensure that the stabbing board pre-job safety check list is completed.
                       -Rig up the liner running unit and have all tools on rig floor -use thread lock the
                      bottom four connections (shoe joint box, spacer joint box, float collar box and
                         spacer joint box above float collar). Make up landing collar one joint above float
                              collar.
                            -Install one (1) 4 m X 7” 29 ppf, L-80, Premium connection pup joint as indicated
                           in the liner running tally sheet to be used for perforation space out purposes.

                   7.    Run 7" liner allowing for a 3 m rat hole.

                   8.    Check the float valves in the shoe by observing that the pipe does not fill up
                       through the shoe, and that when filled from the top, mud will flow out of the shoe.
                    Check for correct mud displacement/casing fill during running. Ensure the mud
                        loggers are notified on commencement of liner running operations and any losses
                      observed are communicated to the Driller and Drilling Supervisor immediately.

                   9.     Liner, filling every five joints. Care to be taken due to the Chrome Material of the
                       Liner when making up the centralizers. The centralizers should be fitted across the
                    body of CSG.
                 10.    Pick up the hanger assembly with the drill pipe elevator, and make up to the top
                          Joint of the liner, start the pin thread on the hanger assembly with chain tongs or
                          strap wrench to avoid damage to the hanger pin thread. Ensure all connections and
                       running tool is correctly made up before pulling the slips and running the
                      assembly through the rotary table. Run assembly carefully through the BOP's.

                  Drilling Program of Vertical Well(Learning Well)
                             SPD18B-08




     REV.: 01                          Doc. No.: DDC-12-14-RE-005-01
DATE: 26 FEB. 2013                      Contract No.: POGC 717-90-35                 Page 140 of 169


                 11.    Fully fill the liner and leaving the rotary slips set on the liner joint pick up 1‐2m to
                      check if the top‐set running tool is fully engaged in the top‐set hanger.

                 12.   Check liner hanger for any damage or leakage during circulation and note liner
                        weight. Lower hanger assembly through the rotary and set the drill pipe slips on
                         the 5” lift nipple. Do not set slips on the hanger assembly. Be careful to keep the
                      hanger centered while lowering through the table to avoid damage to the slips, etc.
                 13.    Circulate liner contents + 20% for obstructions and to remove air from the liner.
                       Limit circulation pressure to 850 psi or 6 bpm maximum. Record Liner Hanger
                       pick up and slack off weights.
                 14.   Run the liner in the well at a rate of 1‐1/2 minutes per stand. Pass drill pipe stands
                       with proper size of Drift. Take particular care as the hanger assembly goes
                       through the B.O.P. stack and 10-3/4” X 9-5/8” cross over. Fill the pipe regularly
                     and run a maximum of 5 stands before filling. Fill every 5 stands and monitor
                         returns closely for indications of losses.
                 15.    Stop at the casing shoe and break circulation maximum pressure 500 psi.
                 16.   When running in open hole, fill up the drill pipe on the fly while going up to
                       another stand (Do not stop in open hole to fill up the drill pipe). Maximum run in
                      speed 2 minutes per stand. Run in slowly to place the shoe approximately 3 m
                     from bottom.
           NOTE: If it is necessary to rotate through a bridge in open hole, break circulation and
                        begin rotating at 10‐12 RPM. (Rotation depends on liner HGR design and should
                      be confirmed by liner HGR engineer). Prior to running liner on ‐wiper trip, if a
                         torque to turn the drill pipe at the shoe ‐was established, this torque reading can
                      be used to calculate the maximum drill down torque. This calculation is done by
                       adding the torques requires rotating the drill pipe at the shoe and adding the
                         torque limit of the Liner Hanger assembly. If a torque value ‐was not established
                        during the ‐wiper trip, limit the torque to the torque limit of the Liner Hanger
                        assembly.

                 17.   Having run the last joint of pipe pick up the cementing manifold and torque all
                       connections using the top drive. Wash down to tag bottom (no more than 4 tones)
                     and mark the pipe. Remember to drift any pups or singles picked up to space out.
                    Take up and down weights. Ensure not to exceed the liner make up torque. Do not
                  move the pipe without circulation unless packing off occurs.
                 18.    Increase circulation slowly to a maximum 75% of hydraulic hanger setting
                         pressure. Reciprocate and condition the hole as required.
                 19.    Circulate bottoms‐up + 10% or until background gas is back down to normal
                          levels (whichever comes last) up 75% of hydraulic hanger setting pressure. Watch
                          for losses, especially if ECD is greater than that whilst drilling.
                 20.   Take SCR’s and pick‐up / slack‐off weights.
                 21.   Make sure liner HGR setting slip not set across 9-5/8” CSG connections.

                  Drilling Program of Vertical Well(Learning Well)

     REV.: 01                          Doc. No.: DDC-12-14-RE-005-01
DATE: 26 FEB. 2013                      Contract No.: POGC 717-90-35                 Page 141 of 169


                    22.11.1     PRODUCTION LINER SETTING PROCEDURES


                            1.    When circulation  is complete: Stop pumps; drop the 1.75" setting
                                    ball in the string.
                            2.    NOTE: When dropping the ball the positive ball dropping sub, verify
                                    that  the  ball  is  installed by  trying  to  rotate  to  handle  counter
                              clockwise (left hand). If the ball is installed, the handle will not turn.
                              Next, rotate the handle one complete revolution clockwise (right hand)
                            and ball will drop. Verify the ball has dropped by rotating the handle
                               counter clockwise (left hand) two revolutions.
                            3.      Pull up  to your pick up weight, mark  pipe. Pick up 2m above
                               previous mark and again mark pipe.
                                Re-establish  circulation, pumping  the  setting  ball down  with  a
                         maximum pump rate of 3 bbl/min for 5 minutes, then let the Ball
                                      free fall. After 20 minutes of Free Fall, start up pumps to check if
                                       ball is on seat.
                            4.     Chain the brake and watch the pressure gauge closely.
                            5.    To set liner hanger:
                    When ball lands slow pumps and increase the pressure to 1,400 psi and hold
                           for 5 minutes.
                     Slack off running string while holding pressure, liner weight should be lost
                            before the shoe reaches bottom.
            NOTE: If 1,400 psi does not set the hanger, pick up to the original position and
                             increase pressure in 200 psi increments, checking for a set after each
                              increase.  If the hanger  is not  set when 2000 psi applied pick up an
                              additional 2m from original setting depth (to reposition hanger slips) and
                             repeat setting sequence.
                       -      When liner is hung set down the entire weight of the liner onto the liner
                          hanger bleed off pressure.
                       -         Set down an additional 15 tons of drill pipe weight.
            NOTE: Setting tool must be in compression for proper operation of the hydraulic
                               release. Do not allow the liner running tool to reach the neutral point under
                            pressure  prior  to  verifying  that  the hanger  slips  are  holding. Hole
                            conditions may require movement of the drill pipe to "work" the set down
                          weight down to the setting tool.
            NOTE: The ball seat will shear at (+/- 15%) 3,300 psi. If the ball seat is sheared out
                         and the hanger slips have not set, the liner can set on bottom and cemented


        This Document Is The Sole Property Of D.D.C. All Information Contained Herein May Neither Be Reproduced,Used,
         Disclosed, Transmitted Or In Any Way Made Public Without The Written Permission Of The D.D.C.

                  Drilling Program of Vertical Well(Learning Well)
                             SPD18B-08




     REV.: 01                          Doc. No.: DDC-12-14-RE-005-01
DATE: 26 FEB. 2013                      Contract No.: POGC 717-90-35                 Page 142 of 169


                              in place. To leave liner on bottom, the liner running tool would need to be
                             released by the mechanical release procedure.
                    6.        Releasing setting tool:
                               While maintaining 15 tons set down weight; increase pressure
                              in work string to 2900 psi to release the setting  tool. Hold for two
                          minutes and then bleed off pressure.
                               Pick up to the neutral weight plus 1mtr to ensure that the setting
                              tool has released from the liner. Do not exceed 2 m of movement at the
                             setting tool  (to  insure  the  slick  stinger  stays  in  the RSB pack  off
                           assembly).  If setting tool has not released, place setting tool back in
                           15tons compression and increase pressure in 200 psi increments, checking
                              for release after each increase.  If tool has not released when Landing
                            Collar shears out, proceed to mechanical release procedure below.
            NOTE: A 5 ton over pull should be noticed when picking up the work string as pins
                            within the Top Set Setting tool are sheared.
                            After setting tool is released, increase pressure to shear out the Landing
                               collar ball seat (3,300 psi), re-establish circulation. If the setting tool does
                            not release hydraulically, right-hand (clockwise) torque will have to be
                            applied to the tool. The torque acts to shear brass screws. The torque
                           value used  in  the setting tool can be adjusted depending in the liner
                           connection make up torque and hole deviation. Bleed off pump pressure
                         and slack off enough weight to ensure the tool is in compression, then
                           apply left-hand torque as required to shear screws. After the screws have
                             sheared, the body of the setting tool will turn 1/4 turn and drop down 1-
                               3/4". Pick straight up 1m to ensure the setting tool has released, and to
                              release the collet from the profile in the liner top (Note: 5 tons of over pull
                           should be seen when picking up the work string). Do not exceed 2 m of
                          pick up at the setting tool. Set down 15 tons of set down weight, and
                            condition hole for cementing.


              22.12.            CASING SET UP

                        The recommended 7" liner  is 29 LBS/FT, L-80 with Vam Top or equivalent

                             connections down to the section TD. One joints below liner hanger is 26LBS/FT,

                     28% CRA. Top of liner shall feature an arrangement in order to allow for mono

                            bore 7" production string.

         Disclosed, Transmitted Or In Any Way Made Public Without The Written Permission Of The D.D.C.

                  Drilling Program of Vertical Well(Learning Well)

     REV.: 01                          Doc. No.: DDC-12-14-RE-005-01

                    Shoe track:

                           7” float shoe (L-80); 1 joints of 7” casing; 7” float collar; 1 joint of 7” casing; Ball

                             Catcher / Landing Collar.


              22.13.            CASING ACCESSORIES

                               All Centralizers to be installed on the body of CSG with Stop collar inside

                               the Centralizers.

                              Thread Lock Float shoe , float collar & LDC

                               Install one bow Centralizer on the first 3 joints of CSG.

                              One bow centralizer every 3 joints in the open hole.

                               Install 1 centralizer every 5 joints in Cased Hole.

                              1 Rigid centralizer below liner HGR

                              1 Stop Collar should be installed inside each centralizers on the Body of

                              CSG(install stop collars on the casing’s prior to PU casing joints to the rig floor).


              22.14.         PRODUCTION LINER CEMENTING PROCEDURES

                               Rig up cement lines to cement swivel and test lines at +/- 5,000 psi.
                               Ensure the following preparation is undertaken prior to cementing:
                               Confirm the slurry volume calculations independently by the
                                      drilling Supervisor and the Cementer. The final decision on the
                            amount of excess cement depends on caliper log(  If caliper log not
                                run in the open hole 70 % excess cement to be considered)
                                Notify  the mud  loggers  and mud  engineer  of  slurry  and
                                displacement volumes.
                             A 100 bbl batch tank will be sent to the rig for pre-mixing of cement
                                       slurry. If the cement slurry volume is no more than 100 bbl
                                  consideration should be given batch mixing the slurry volume it to
                                 ensure good density control.



        This Document Is The Sole Property Of D.D.C. All Information Contained Herein May Neither Be Reproduced,Used,
         Disclosed, Transmitted Or In Any Way Made Public Without The Written Permission Of The D.D.C.


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 144 از 181 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                  Drilling Program of Vertical Well(Learning Well)
                             SPD18B-08




     REV.: 01                          Doc. No.: DDC-12-14-RE-005-01
DATE: 26 FEB. 2013                      Contract No.: POGC 717-90-35                 Page 144 of 169


                               Ensure sufficient overbalance will exist throughout the cement
                                     job. Flush out all the lines on the rig floor. Measure and record the
                            volume of line from the cement unit to the rig floor.
                              Check the mix water transfer lines for leaks. All valves off this
                                      line should be locked closed.
                                 Driller, mud engineers and mud loggers to record all pit levels
                               immediately prior to cementing operations commencing.
                            2.     Close drill pipe safety valve and apply 200 psi standpipe pressure.
                             Monitor pressure throughout cement job. Open Lo-Torque valve. Flag
                          Sub Indicator to be in Horizontal Position. Release both plugs (2 bbl
                                  apart) after pumping cement. Watch for indication of pump down plugs
                               passing through flag sub.

                               Release second pump down plug by rotating lever on 'TD' plug
                               dropping headcounter clockwise (left hand). Watch for indication of
                         pump down plug passing through flag sub.
                       NOTE: Prior to releasing 2nd pump down plug. Flag Sub Indicator
                                 has to be in Horizontal Position.
                            3.    Pump 10 bbls fresh water.
                                        -  Open drill pipe safety valve, close Lo-Torque valve
                                        -   Displace with cement unit at the optimum rate.
                                        -  Watch for indication of drill pipe plug latching into liner wiper
                                 plug  on  pressure  gauge.  If  this  pressure  indication  is  seen,
                                      recalculate  total  displacement volume. The new  displacement
                              volume will be the actual displacement volume for the drill pipe
                                    plus the calculated liner volume. If liner wiper plug shear was not
                                     seen, pump to calculated volume only.
                                        -  Slow pump rate 5-10 bbl before total displacement.
                                        -   First plugs lands in landing collar pressure will increase, continue
                                      to displace until the second plug lands with 1,000 psi over final
                                    displacing  pressure, 2,000 psi is adequate. If plug does not land
                          pump over displace no more than 1/2 of the shoe track volume.
                                        -  Bleed off pressure and check for back flow. a. If plug as landed:
                                      Pressure up to 500 psi on the drill string.
                                       Pull out with running tool. A loss of pressure when the
                                         stuffing box clears the hanger will be noted.

────────────────────────────────────────────
                  Drilling Program of Vertical Well(Learning Well)
                             SPD18B-08




     REV.: 01                          Doc. No.: DDC-12-14-RE-005-01
DATE: 26 FEB. 2013                      Contract No.: POGC 717-90-35                 Page 145 of 169


                                    POOH 5 Stands and reverse circulate
                                    -    When pressure is lost pick up and additional 1m. b. If plug has not
                                landed:
                                       Pull out with running tool plus one stand.
                                      Reverse circulate drill pipe capacity or until cement returns are
                                    observed at surface. The mud engineer shall monitor the returns for
                                        the first sign of WBM contaminated with spacer, which needs to
                                  be diverted overboard. Under no circumstances is spacer to be
                                mixed with the surface active mud system.
                                4. Pull out of hole. Do not rotate pipe when pulling out of hole.
                                5. Lay down liner setting tool, surface equipment, singles and any pup
                                  joints.

         Detailed procedure of Liner HGR Setting and cementing will advise in due time

              22.15.             LINER PRESS TEST

                        If plug bumped Liner to be tested to 3000 psi for 10 minutes.


              22.16.           LINER CLEAN OUT


                       •  RIH 8-1/2” insert bit rotary BHA to drill cement to top of liner.

                       •  Make up and RIH 5-7/8” insert bit with 7” and 9-5/8” casing scrapers
                        tandem  assembly. Clean cement inside liner and scrap to top of liner
                            landing collar.

                       •  Pressure test casing and liner lap up to 4000 psi.


            Note: Completion Program, Completion Fluid and Well
                Testing Program will advise in due time.
